<file path="src/lib/utils.ts">
import { clsx, type ClassValue } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

/**
 * Combines multiple class values into a single className string
 * with proper Tailwind CSS class merging.
 * 
 * @param inputs - Class values to merge
 * @returns Merged className string
 */
export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

/**
 * Utility function to generate a unique ID.
 * Useful for ensuring unique IDs for form elements.
 * 
 * @param prefix - Optional prefix for the ID
 * @returns A unique ID string
 */
export function generateId(prefix = 'id'): string {
  const randomId = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9);
  return `${prefix}-${randomId}`;
}

/**
 * Type guard to check if a value is not null or undefined
 * 
 * @param value - Value to check
 * @returns True if value is not null or undefined
 */
export function isNotNullOrUndefined<T>(value: T | null | undefined): value is T {
  return value !== null && value !== undefined;
}

/**
 * Returns only the props that are safe to spread onto a DOM element
 * by filtering out custom props
 * 
 * @param props - The props object
 * @param propNames - Names of custom props to filter out
 * @returns Object with only DOM-safe props
 */
export function filterDOMProps<T extends Record<string, any>, K extends keyof T>(
  props: T, 
  propNames: K[]
): Omit<T, K> {
  const result = { ...props };
  propNames.forEach(name => delete result[name]);
  return result;
}
</file>

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { ThemeProvider as NextThemesProvider } from "next-themes"
import type { ThemeProviderProps } from "next-themes/dist/types"

/**
 * ThemeProvider component for handling theme state
 * 
 * This wraps the next-themes provider with sensible defaults
 * for handling light/dark themes and system preferences
 */
export function ThemeProvider({ 
  children, 
  ...props 
}: ThemeProviderProps) {
  return (
    <NextThemesProvider
      attribute="class"
      defaultTheme="system"
      enableSystem
      disableTransitionOnChange
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </NextThemesProvider>
  )
}

<file path="src/components/ui/button.tsx">
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { ThemeColor, ComponentVariant } from "@/lib/theme-utils"
import { BUTTON_COLORS, type ButtonVariant } from "@/styles/constants/button-colors"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "float-button inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-[4px] text-sm font-medium ring-offset-background transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary: "bg-[var(--float-button-primary-bg)] text-[var(--float-button-primary-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-primary-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-primary-fg-hover)] border-[var(--float-button-primary-border)] hover:border-[var(--float-button-primary-border-hover)]",
        secondary: "bg-[var(--float-button-secondary-bg)] text-[var(--float-button-secondary-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-secondary-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-secondary-fg-hover)] border-[var(--float-button-secondary-border)] hover:border-[var(--float-button-secondary-border-hover)]",
        ghost: "text-[var(--float-button-ghost-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-ghost-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-ghost-fg-hover)]",
        outline: "border  text-[var(--float-button-outline-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-outline-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-outline-fg-hover)] border-[var(--float-button-outline-border)] hover:border-[var(--float-button-outline-border-hover)]",
        link: "text-[var(--float-button-link-fg)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-link-fg-hover)] bg-transparent border-transparent",
        destructive: "bg-[var(--float-button-destructive-bg)] text-[var(--float-button-destructive-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-destructive-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-destructive-fg-hover)] border-[var(--float-button-destructive-border)] hover:border-[var(--float-button-destructive-border-hover)]",
        // Backward compatibility
        default: "bg-[var(--float-button-primary-bg)] text-[var(--float-button-primary-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-primary-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-primary-fg-hover)] border-[var(--float-button-primary-border)] hover:border-[var(--float-button-primary-border-hover)]",
        tertiary: "text-[var(--float-button-ghost-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-ghost-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-ghost-fg-hover)]",
        primaryError: "bg-[var(--float-button-destructive-bg)] text-[var(--float-button-destructive-fg)] hover:bg-[var(--float-button-destructive-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--float-button-destructive-fg-hover)] border-[var(--float-button-destructive-border)] hover:border-[var(--float-button-destructive-border-hover)]",
      },
      size: {
        sm: "h-6 px-2 text-xs float-button-sm",
        md: "h-8 px-3 text-sm float-button-md",
        lg: "h-10 px-5 text-base float-button-lg",
        iconSm: "h-6 w-6 p-0 float-button-icon-sm",
        iconMd: "h-8 w-8 p-0 float-button-icon-md",
        iconLg: "h-10 w-10 p-0 float-button-icon-lg",
        // For backward compatibility
        icon: "h-8 w-8 p-0 float-button-icon-md", // Map "icon" to be equivalent to iconMd
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "primary",
      size: "md",
    },
  }
)

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
    Omit<VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants>, 'variant'> {
  asChild?: boolean;
  /**
   * Icon to display before the button text
   */
  leftIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Icon to display after the button text
   */
  rightIcon?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Whether the button is in a loading state
   */
  isLoading?: boolean;
  /**
   * Theme color to apply to the button (uses design system colors)
   */
  color?: ThemeColor;
  /**
   * Semantic design system variant - use this instead of variant for design system consistency
   */
  dsVariant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'outline' | 'link' | 'destructive' | 'default' | 'tertiary' | 'primaryError';
  /**
   * Whether the button should only show an icon
   */
  iconOnly?: boolean;
  /**
   * Button visual style variant
   */
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'outline' | 'link' | 'destructive' | 'default' | 'tertiary' | 'primaryError';
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ 
    className, 
    variant, 
    size = "md", 
    asChild = false, 
    leftIcon, 
    rightIcon, 
    isLoading = false,
    color = "brand",
    dsVariant,
    iconOnly = false,
    children,
    ...props 
  }, ref) => {
    // Map deprecated variants to canonical ones
    let resolvedVariant = dsVariant || variant;
    if (resolvedVariant === "default") resolvedVariant = "primary";
    if (resolvedVariant === "tertiary") resolvedVariant = "ghost";
    if (resolvedVariant === "primaryError") resolvedVariant = "destructive";

    const isDisabled = props.disabled || isLoading;
    
    // Choose icon size variant for iconOnly buttons
    let sizeVariant = size;
    if (iconOnly) {
      // If already an icon size, keep it; otherwise convert regular size to icon size
      if (typeof size === 'string' && !size.startsWith('icon')) {
        if (size === "sm") sizeVariant = "iconSm";
        else if (size === "lg") sizeVariant = "iconLg";
        else sizeVariant = "iconMd";
      }
    }

    const classes = cn(
      buttonVariants({ 
        variant: resolvedVariant, 
        size: sizeVariant, 
        className 
      })
    );

    const content = (
      <>
        {leftIcon && !asChild && (
          <span className="flex items-center justify-center">
            {leftIcon}
          </span>
        )}
        {isLoading && !asChild ? (
          <span className="flex items-center">
            <svg className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-2" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1"></circle>
              <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
            </svg>
            <span>Loading...</span>
          </span>
        ) : (
          children
        )}
        {rightIcon && !asChild && (
          <span className="flex items-center justify-center">
            {rightIcon}
          </span>
        )}
      </>
    );

    if (asChild) {
      return (
        <Slot 
          className={classes} 
          ref={ref as any}
          {...props}
        >
          {children}
        </Slot>
      );
    }

    return (
      <button
        className={classes}
        disabled={isDisabled}
        ref={ref}
        {...props}
      >
        {content}
      </button>
    );
  }
);

Button.displayName = "Button";

export { Button, buttonVariants };
</file>

<file path="src/components/ui/org-switcher.tsx">
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { ChevronsUpDown, Plus } from "lucide-react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

import { DropdownMenu } from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"
import {
  SidebarMenu,
  SidebarMenuButton,
  SidebarMenuItem,
  useSidebar,
} from "@/components/ui/sidebar"

export interface Org {
  id: string
  name: string
  logo: React.ElementType
  plan?: string
}

interface OrgSwitcherProps {
  organizations: Org[]
}

export function OrgSwitcher({ organizations }: OrgSwitcherProps) {
  const { isMobile } = useSidebar()
  const [activeOrg, setActiveOrg] = React.useState(organizations[0])

  if (!activeOrg) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <SidebarMenu className="org-switcher-root" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <SidebarMenuItem>
        <DropdownMenu.Root>
          <DropdownMenu.Trigger asChild>
            <SidebarMenuButton
              size="lg"
              className={
                cn(
                  "border border-sidebar-border rounded-md focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-sidebar-accent focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-[var(--float-sidebar-bg)]",
                  "group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:border-0",
                  "group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:w-10 group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:h-10",
                  "data-[state=open]:bg-sidebar-accent data-[state=open]:text-sidebar-accent-foreground"
                )
              }
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 flex-shrink-0 flex-grow-0 rounded-[4px] bg-secondary text-sidebar-primary-foreground">
                <activeOrg.logo className="w-full h-full rounded-[2px] object-contain" />
              </div>
              <div
                className="transition-all duration-200 max-w-[120px] opacity-100 ml-2 group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:max-w-0 group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:opacity-0 group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:ml-0 overflow-hidden whitespace-nowrap grid flex-1 text-left text-sm leading-tight"
              >
                <span className="truncate font-semibold">
                  {activeOrg.name}
                </span>
                {activeOrg.plan && (
                  <span className="truncate text-xs">{activeOrg.plan}</span>
                )}
              </div>
              <ChevronsUpDown
                className="ml-auto transition-all duration-200 opacity-100 group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:max-w-0 group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:opacity-0 group-data-[state=collapsed]/sidebar:ml-0 overflow-hidden"
              />
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </DropdownMenu.Trigger>
          <DropdownMenu.Content
            className="w-[--radix-dropdown-menu-trigger-width] min-w-56 rounded-lg"
            align="start"
            side={isMobile ? "bottom" : "right"}
            sideOffset={4}
          >
            <DropdownMenu.Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Organizations
            </DropdownMenu.Label>
            {organizations.map((org, index) => (
              <DropdownMenu.Item
                key={org.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveOrg(org)}
                className="gap-2 p-2"
              >
                <div className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-sm border">
                  <org.logo className="size-4 shrink-0" />
                </div>
                {org.name}
                <DropdownMenu.Shortcut>⌘{index + 1}</DropdownMenu.Shortcut>
              </DropdownMenu.Item>
            ))}
            <DropdownMenu.Separator />
            <DropdownMenu.Item className="gap-2 p-2">
              <div className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md border bg-background">
                <Plus className="size-4" />
              </div>
              <div className="font-medium text-muted-foreground">Add organization</div>
            </DropdownMenu.Item>
          </DropdownMenu.Content>
        </DropdownMenu.Root>
      </SidebarMenuItem>
    </SidebarMenu>
  )
}

<file path="src/app/design-system/page.tsx">
// src/app/design-system/page.tsx
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useTheme } from "@/themes/theme-context";
import { Button, buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";

export default function DesignSystemPage() {
  const { isDark, setTheme } = useTheme();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  
  // Initialize on mount - client-side only
  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);
  
  // Function to toggle dark mode
  const toggleDarkMode = () => {
    setTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark');
  };

  // shadcn colors for swatches with descriptions
  const shadcnColors = [
    { name: 'primary', desc: 'Primary button color' },
    { name: 'primary-foreground', desc: 'Primary button text' },
    { name: 'secondary', desc: 'Secondary button color' },
    { name: 'secondary-foreground', desc: 'Secondary button text' },
    { name: 'accent', desc: 'Accent color' },
    { name: 'accent-foreground', desc: 'Accent text' },
    { name: 'muted', desc: 'Muted background' },
    { name: 'muted-foreground', desc: 'Muted text' },
    { name: 'destructive', desc: 'Destructive action color' },
    { name: 'destructive-foreground', desc: 'Destructive text' },
    { name: 'card', desc: 'Card background' },
    { name: 'card-foreground', desc: 'Card text' },
    { name: 'popover', desc: 'Popover background' },
    { name: 'popover-foreground', desc: 'Popover text' },
    { name: 'border', desc: 'Border color' },
    { name: 'input', desc: 'Input border' },
    { name: 'ring', desc: 'Focus ring' },
    { name: 'background', desc: 'Page background' },
    { name: 'foreground', desc: 'Default text' }
  ];

  // Function to render a color swatch with inline styles
  const renderSwatch = (type: string, weight: number) => {
    return (
      <div key={`${type}-${weight}`} className="mb-4">
        <div 
          style={{ 
            backgroundColor: `var(--color-${type}-${weight})`,
            borderRadius: '0.375rem',
            height: '6rem',
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
            padding: '0.5rem'
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ 
            color: weight <= 400 ? 'var(--color-gray-900)' : 'var(--color-gray-50)',
            fontSize: '0.75rem',
            fontWeight: '500'
          }}>
            {type}-{weight}
          </div>
          <div style={{ 
            color: weight <= 400 ? 'var(--color-gray-900)' : 'var(--color-gray-50)',
            fontSize: '0.75rem',
            opacity: 0.7
          }}>
            Aa
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  // Function to get computed CSS variable value
  const [cssVarValues, setCssVarValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && mounted) {
      const values: Record<string, string> = {};
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        shadcnColors.forEach(color => {
          const styles = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
          const value = styles.getPropertyValue(`--${color.name}`);
          values[color.name] = value.trim();
        });
        
        setCssVarValues(values);
      }, 100);
    }
  }, [isDark, mounted, shadcnColors]);

  // Don't render until client-side to avoid hydration mismatch
  if (!mounted) return null;

  return (

      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Float Colors</h1>
      </div>
      
      {/* Theme Information */}
      <section className="mb-8 p-4 bg-bg-secondary border border-border-primary rounded-md">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-2">Current Theme: {isDark ? 'dark' : 'light'}</h2>
        <p className="mb-4">
          This design system uses your custom Tailwind semantic color classes.
        </p>
        
        {/* Test box with direct classes */}
        <div className={`p-2 border rounded ${isDark ? 'bg-gray-800 text-white' : 'bg-white text-black'}`}>
          This box uses direct classes based on the isDark state
        </div>
        
        {/* Test box with Tailwind dark mode */}

          {[25, 50, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900, 950].map((weight) => 
            renderSwatch('gray', weight)
          )}
        </div>

        <h3 className="text-lg font-medium mb-3">Brand Colors</h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-6 gap-2 mb-6">

<file path="tailwind.config.js">
/**
 * Color System Overview
 * ====================
 * 
 * This design system uses a property-namespace pattern to organize semantic colors.
 * Colors are organized by their purpose (text, background, border, etc.) and then
 * by their semantic role (primary, secondary, error, etc.).
 * 
 * Structure:
 * ----------
 * colors: {
 *   // Standard colors (overriding Tailwind defaults)
 *   gray: { 25: "var(--color-gray-25)", 50: "var(--color-gray-50)", ... },
 *   red: { ... }, // maps to error colors
 *   amber: { ... }, // maps to warning colors
 *   green: { ... }, // maps to success colors
 *   indigo: { ... }, // maps to brand colors
 *   
 *   // Semantic color groups (in theme.extend.colors)
 *   bg: { primary: "var(--bg-primary)", secondary: "var(--bg-secondary)", ... },
 *   text: { primary: "var(--text-primary)", secondary: "var(--text-secondary)", ... },
 *   border: { primary: "var(--border-primary)", ... },
 *   fg: { primary: "var(--fg-primary)", ... }
 * }
 * 
 * How it works:
 * -------------
 * 1. CSS variables are defined in globals.css with light/dark mode variants
 * 2. These variables are referenced in tailwind.config.js
 * 3. Tailwind generates utility classes based on this configuration
 * 
 * Usage examples:
 * --------------
 * - bg-bg-primary → applies var(--bg-primary) as background color
 * - text-text-secondary → applies var(--text-secondary) as text color
 * - border-border-error → applies var(--border-error) as border color
 * 
 * The property-namespace pattern (e.g., bg-bg-primary) avoids conflicts with
 * other utility classes while maintaining semantic meaning.
 * 
 * 
 * Visual representation:
 * 
 * ┌─ tailwind.config.js ─────────────┐        ┌─ globals.css ─────────────────┐
 * │                                  │        │                                │
 * │ colors: {                        │        │ :root {                        │
 * │   bg: {                          │        │   --bg-primary: #ffffff;       │
 * │     primary: "var(--bg-primary)" │───┐    │   --bg-secondary: #fafafa;     │
 * │   },                             │   │    │   --text-primary: #1a1a1e;     │
 * │   text: {                        │   │    │   ...                          │
 * │     primary: "var(--text-primary)"│──┼────│→ }                             │
 * │   }                              │   │    │                                │
 * │ }                                │   │    │ .dark {                        │
 * └──────────────────────────────────┘   │    │   --bg-primary: #131316;       │
 *                                        │    │   --text-primary: #fcfcfc;     │
 * ┌─ Generated CSS ───────────────────┐  │    │   ...                          │
 * │                                   │  │    │ }                              │
 * │ .bg-bg-primary {                  │←─┘    └────────────────────────────────┘
 * │   background-color: var(--bg-primary);│    
 * │ }                                 │      ┌─ HTML Usage ─────────────────┐
 * │ .text-text-primary {              │      │                              │
 * │   color: var(--text-primary);     │      │ <div class="bg-bg-primary    │
 * │ }                                 │      │            text-text-primary">│
 * └───────────────────────────────────┘      │   Hello World                │
 *                                            │ </div>                        │
 *                                            └──────────────────────────────┘
 */
/** @type {import('tailwindcss').Config} */
module.exports = {
  darkMode: ["class"],
  content: [
    './src/pages/**/*.{ts,tsx}',
    './src/components/**/*.{ts,tsx}',
    './src/app/**/*.{ts,tsx}',
    './src/**/*.{ts,tsx}',
  ],
  theme: {
  	colors: {
  		transparent: 'transparent',
  		current: 'currentColor',
  		black: 'var(--color-black)',
  		white: 'var(--color-white)',
  		background: 'hsl(var(--background))',
  		foreground: 'hsl(var(--foreground))',
  		card: 'hsl(var(--card))',
  		'card-foreground': 'hsl(var(--card-foreground))',
  		popover: 'hsl(var(--popover))',
  		'popover-foreground': 'hsl(var(--popover-foreground))',
  		primary: 'hsl(var(--primary))',
  		'primary-foreground': 'hsl(var(--primary-foreground))',
  		secondary: 'hsl(var(--secondary))',
  		'secondary-foreground': 'hsl(var(--secondary-foreground))',
  		muted: 'hsl(var(--muted))',
  		'muted-foreground': 'hsl(var(--muted-foreground))',
  		accent: 'hsl(var(--accent))',
  		'accent-foreground': 'hsl(var(--accent-foreground))',

                  </ContextMenuItem>

                    <Share className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                    Share
                  </ContextMenuItem>
                  <ContextMenuSeparator />
                  <ContextMenuItem color="error">
                    <LogOut className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                    Delete
                  </ContextMenuItem>
                </ContextMenuContent>
              </ContextMenu>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Context Menu with Submenu</CardTitle>
              <CardDescription>Right-click to open nested menus</CardDescription>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <ContextMenu>
                <ContextMenuTrigger className="flex h-[150px] w-full items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-dashed text-sm">
                  Right click here for nested menu
                </ContextMenuTrigger>
                <ContextMenuContent>
                  <ContextMenuItem>
                    <User className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                    View Profile
                  </ContextMenuItem>
                  <ContextMenuSub>
                    <ContextMenuSubTrigger>
                      <Settings className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                      Settings
                    </ContextMenuSubTrigger>
                    <ContextMenuSubContent>
                      <ContextMenuItem>Account</ContextMenuItem>
                      <ContextMenuItem>Preferences</ContextMenuItem>
                      <ContextMenuItem>Notifications</ContextMenuItem>
                    </ContextMenuSubContent>
                  </ContextMenuSub>
                </ContextMenuContent>
              </ContextMenu>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <section className="mb-12">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-4">Tabs</h2>
        <Tabs defaultValue="account" className="w-full">
          <TabsList>
            <TabsTrigger value="account">Account</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="password">Password</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="settings">Settings</TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>
          <TabsContent value="account">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Account</CardTitle>
                <CardDescription>
                  Make changes to your account settings.
                </CardDescription>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label htmlFor="name">Name</Label>
                  <Input id="name" defaultValue="John Doe" />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
                  <Input id="email" type="email" defaultValue="john@example.com" />
                </div>
              </CardContent>
              <CardFooter>
                <Button>Save changes</Button>
              </CardFooter>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="password">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Password</CardTitle>
                <CardDescription>
                  Change your password here.
                </CardDescription>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label htmlFor="current">Current password</Label>
                  <Input id="current" type="password" />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label htmlFor="new">New password</Label>
                  <Input id="new" type="password" />
                </div>
              </CardContent>
              <CardFooter>
                <Button>Change password</Button>
              </CardFooter>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="settings">
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle>Settings</CardTitle>
                <CardDescription>
                  Manage your application settings.
                </CardDescription>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                  <Switch id="notifications" />
                  <Label htmlFor="notifications">Enable notifications</Label>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                  <Switch id="marketing" />
                  <Label htmlFor="marketing">Enable marketing emails</Label>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
              <CardFooter>
                <Button>Save preferences</Button>
              </CardFooter>
            </Card>
          </TabsContent>
        </Tabs>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

/**
 * Global CSS for Design System
 * 
 * This file defines the color system based on our Figma design tokens.
 * It follows a layered approach:
 * 1. Foundational Colors: Raw color values from design system
 * 2. Semantic Variables: Meaningful variables that map to foundational colors
 * 3. shadcn Variables: Variables required by shadcn components
 * 4. Component-specific Variables: Imported from component CSS files
 *
 * This structure allows changes at any level without breaking the entire system.
 */

/* REMOVE BODY TEST STYLES */
/*

  border: 20px solid lime !important;
  background-color: orange !important;
}
*/

  --float-badge-gray-bg: var(--float-button-secondary-bg);
  --float-badge-gray-fg: var(--float-button-secondary-fg);
  --float-badge-gray-border: var(--float-button-secondary-border);
  --float-badge-gray-bg-hover: var(--float-button-secondary-bg-hover);
  --float-badge-gray-fg-hover: var(--float-button-secondary-fg-hover);
  --float-badge-gray-border-hover: var(--float-button-secondary-border-hover);

  /* Destructive Badge (mimics Button Destructive) */
  --float-badge-destructive-bg: var(--float-button-destructive-bg);
  --float-badge-destructive-fg: var(--float-button-destructive-fg);
  --float-badge-destructive-border: var(--float-button-destructive-border);
  --float-badge-destructive-bg-hover: var(--float-button-destructive-bg-hover);
  --float-badge-destructive-fg-hover: var(--float-button-destructive-fg-hover);
  --float-badge-destructive-border-hover: var(--float-button-destructive-border-hover);

  /* Ghost Badge (mimics Button Ghost) */
  --float-badge-ghost-fg: var(--float-button-ghost-fg);
  --float-badge-ghost-bg-hover: var(--float-button-ghost-bg-hover);
  --float-badge-ghost-fg-hover: var(--float-button-ghost-fg-hover);
  /* Ghost badges typically don't have a border in their resting state */
  --float-badge-ghost-border: transparent; 
  --float-badge-ghost-border-hover: transparent;


  /* Outline Badge (mimics Button Outline) */
  --float-badge-outline-bg: transparent; /* Outline badges are typically transparent */
  --float-badge-outline-fg: var(--float-button-outline-fg);
  --float-badge-outline-border: var(--float-button-outline-border);
  --float-badge-outline-bg-hover: var(--float-button-outline-bg-hover);
  --float-badge-outline-fg-hover: var(--float-button-outline-fg-hover);
  --float-badge-outline-border-hover: var(--float-button-outline-border-hover);
}

/* No specific dark theme overrides needed here if button variables correctly handle dark theme */

/* ---------------------------------------- */
/* Component Specific Variables: Checkbox   */
/* ---------------------------------------- */
:root {
  /* Unchecked State */
  --float-checkbox-border: var(--border-primary);
  --float-checkbox-bg: transparent; /* Typically transparent when unchecked */
  
  /* Checked State */
  --float-checkbox-checked-bg: var(--bg-brand-solid);
  --float-checkbox-checked-border: var(--bg-brand-solid); /* Border often matches background when checked */
  --float-checkbox-checked-indicator: var(--text-brand-primary); /* Checkmark color */
  
  /* Hover State (Unchecked) - Can be subtle or rely on focus ring */
  --float-checkbox-hover-border: var(--border-brand); 

  /* Hover State (Checked) */
  --float-checkbox-checked-hover-bg: var(--bg-brand-solid); /* Might be same as checked or slightly darker/lighter */
  --float-checkbox-checked-hover-border: var(--bg-brand-solid);

  /* Focus State - Uses existing --ring for the ring color */
  
  /* Disabled State (Unchecked) */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-border: var(--border-disabled);
  --float-checkbox-disabled-bg: var(--bg-disabled);

  /* Disabled State (Checked) */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-checked-bg: var(--border-disabled); /* Using border-disabled to appear grayed out */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-checked-border: var(--border-disabled);
  --float-checkbox-disabled-checked-indicator: var(--text-disabled);
}

.dark {
  /* Unchecked State */
  --float-checkbox-border: var(--border-primary); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  --float-checkbox-bg: transparent;
  
  /* Checked State */
  --float-checkbox-checked-bg: var(--bg-brand-solid); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  --float-checkbox-checked-border: var(--bg-brand-solid); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  --float-checkbox-checked-indicator: var(--text-brand-primary); /* Uses dark theme variable */

  /* Hover State (Unchecked) */
  --float-checkbox-hover-border: var(--border-brand); /* Uses dark theme variable */

  /* Hover State (Checked) */
  --float-checkbox-checked-hover-bg: var(--bg-brand-solid); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  --float-checkbox-checked-hover-border: var(--bg-brand-solid); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  
  /* Disabled State (Unchecked) */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-border: var(--border-disabled); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-bg: var(--bg-disabled); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  
  /* Disabled State (Checked) */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-checked-bg: var(--border-disabled); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-checked-border: var(--border-disabled); /* Uses dark theme variable */
  --float-checkbox-disabled-checked-indicator: var(--text-disabled); /* Uses dark theme variable */
}

:root {
  /* Unchecked State */
  --float-radio-border: var(--border-primary);
  --float-radio-bg: transparent;
  
  /* Checked State */
  --float-radio-checked-border: var(--border-brand); /* Often a brand color for the border when checked */
  --float-radio-checked-indicator: var(--fg-brand-primary); /* The inner dot color */
  
  /* Hover State (Unchecked) */
  --float-radio-hover-border: var(--border-brand); 

  /* Hover State (Checked) - Indicator usually doesn't change on hover, border might */
  --float-radio-checked-hover-border: var(--border-brand); /* Or a slightly different shade */

  /* Focus State - Uses existing --ring */
  
  /* Disabled State (Unchecked) */
  --float-radio-disabled-border: var(--border-disabled);
  --float-radio-disabled-bg: var(--bg-disabled);

  /* Disabled State (Checked) */
  --float-radio-disabled-checked-border: var(--border-disabled);
  --float-radio-disabled-checked-indicator: var(--text-disabled);
}

  --float-radio-bg: transparent;
  
  /* Checked State */
  --float-radio-checked-border: var(--border-brand);
  --float-radio-checked-indicator: var(--fg-brand-primary);

  /* Hover State (Checked) */
  --float-radio-checked-hover-border: var(--border-brand);
  
  /* Disabled State (Unchecked) */
  --float-radio-disabled-border: var(--border-disabled);
  --float-radio-disabled-bg: var(--bg-disabled);
  
  /* Disabled State (Checked) */
  --float-radio-disabled-checked-border: var(--border-disabled);
  --float-radio-disabled-checked-indicator: var(--text-disabled);
}

  }

  /* Styling for the SidebarMenu (ul) component from sidebar.tsx */
  /* The component itself applies cn("space-y-1", className) */

  .float-sidebar-menu {
    /* @apply space-y-1; /* This is already on the component */
    @apply px-2; /* This is 8px padding on x-axis for the UL */

    /* Alternatively, manage spacing via elements between ULs */
  }

  /* Sidebar Menu Button */
  .float-sidebar-menu-button {

    /* Base styles are mostly from the component, this class is for potential overrides or specific child targeting */
    @apply flex items-center w-full text-[14px] rounded-md h-9; /* h-9 might need adjustment based on p-2 */
    @apply bg-[var(--float-sidebar-item-bg)] border border-transparent;
    @apply transition-all duration-200;
  }

  /* Icon within the button */
  .float-sidebar-menu-button-icon {
    @apply h-5 w-5 text-[var(--float-sidebar-item-icon-fg)];
    /* margin-right: 8px !important; /* REMOVED - Rely on gap-2 from parent button */
    @apply transition-colors duration-200;
  }

  .float-sidebar-menu-button:hover .float-sidebar-menu-button-icon {
    color: var(--float-sidebar-item-icon-fg-hover) !important;
  }

  /* Text within the button */

    @apply truncate font-normal text-[var(--text-secondary)];

    @apply transition-colors duration-200;
  }

  .float-sidebar-menu-button:hover .float-sidebar-menu-button-text {
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }

    /* pt-8 (32px) to create space above it (below previous UL), pb-2 for space below label before next UL */
  }
